# YAAT — architecture

YAAT is the instructor and RPO desktop client for VATSIM ATC training. Its layers run from `Yaat.Sim` (all aviation logic and the simulation, no UI) through the WASM-clean strip and vTDLS layers and `Yaat.Client.Core` to the `Yaat.Client` desktop app; `Yaat.Sim` references nothing in the repo, and the sibling yaat-server hosts the rooms on top of it. This doc is the entry point: the Task Index, the layers, the integration footguns and where tests live.

Per-file detail lives in each subsystem doc's `## File map`, listed under Deep docs. Terms used in a project sense are in the glossary, [CONTEXT.md](CONTEXT.md).

## Task Index

"I need to change X, which files?" One row per kind of change, files in the order to read them.

| Task | Key files (in order of relevance) |
|------|----------------------------------|
| **Add or change a `wait_until` condition kind (automation pipe)** | `src/Yaat.Client/Automation/Handlers/WaitUntilParams.cs` (kinds, `KindNames`, readers) → `Handlers/WaitUntilHandler.cs` (`Progress.Evaluate`/`EvaluateOne`; the client-log read in the poll loop) → `Handlers/ClientLogTail.cs` → `Automation/IAutomationState.cs` + `MainViewModelAutomationState.cs` → yaat-server `tools/Yaat.ClientDriver.Mcp/Tools/PipeTools.cs` (the hand-written `wait_until` kind list) → yaat-server `docs/client-driver-mcp.md` → tests `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/Automation/AutomationWaitUntilTests.cs`, `AutomationWaitUntilClientLogTests.cs` |
| **Change what `list_windows` reports over the automation pipe (windows, overlay popups, in-window overlays)** | `src/Yaat.Client/Automation/Handlers/ListWindowsHandler.cs` (`AddOverlayPopups`, `AddInWindowOverlays`, `InWindowOverlayClass`) → `Views/MainWindow.axaml` (each in-window overlay's root `Border`: the class, an AutomationId, `AutomationProperties.Name` as the row title) → `Automation/Protocol/WindowInfo.cs` (shared with the MCP by source link; a shape change bumps `ProtocolVersion.cs`) → `Automation/Tree/NodeInfoBuilder.cs` (`GetWindowBounds`, `GetOwnerId`) → yaat-server `tools/Yaat.ClientDriver.Mcp/Pipe/PipeDescribe.cs`, `Tools/InspectTools.cs` (the tool description), `Tools/RecordTools.cs` (popup rows are never recordable), `live-check.ps1` (the row regex, the menu popup count) → tests `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/Automation/AutomationHostTests.cs`, yaat-server `InspectToolsPipeTests.cs` |
| **Change a pause trigger (`arm_pause_triggers`)** | `src/Yaat.Client/Automation/PauseTriggerMonitor.cs` → `Automation/Tools/AutomationTools.Pause.cs` → `Automation/Tools/AutomationTools.Command.cs` (`send_command` marks its own command) → `ViewModels/MainViewModel.cs` (`SubmitCommandTextAsync`, `LastSubmission`) → yaat-server `Simulation/RoomEngine.cs` (`SendCommandAsync`: the Error line answering a command) → test `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/Automation/PauseTriggerMonitorTests.cs` |
| **Add a taxi-family verb (`TTP`, `RTAXI`)** | `Commands/CanonicalCommandType.cs` (append last) → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` (`GroundCommands()`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (the switch) → `Commands/GroundCommandParser.cs` (the parser; `TTP` expands to `TAXI … @GATE` here and needs nothing after); only for a verb that is its own command type: `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` → `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` → the phases' `CanAcceptCommand` → `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs`; tests `GroundCommandParser*Tests`, `Simulation/GroundTaxi/*`, `ClassifyCommandCompletenessTests`, client `ArgumentSuggesterTaxiwayTests`; docs `COMMANDS.md` → `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` |
| **Change how a condition block (`AT`/`LV`/`ATFN`) is parsed or refused** | `Commands/CommandSchemeParser.cs` (`ParseBlockToCanonical`, `EmptyConditionFailure`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ParseBlock`, `ParseAtCondition`, `AtConditionTokenShapeFailure`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/CompoundParseFailureTests.cs` |
| **Accept a new kind of fix name in command arguments** | `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/FixResolutionContext.cs` → `Data/NavigationDatabase.cs` (`GetFixPosition(name, ctx)`, `TryGetTerminalWaypoint`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` / `ApproachCommandParser.cs` / `DepartureCommandParser.cs` → `Data/FrdResolver.cs` → the callers that pass the aircraft's context (`Simulation/Actions/ActionArms.cs`, `ActionRouter.cs`, `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` restore, `CommandQueue.cs` restore, `SimulationEngine.Presets.cs`, `Scenarios/ScenarioValidator.cs`, client `Services/VfrCommandGate.cs` and `Commands/CommandSchemeParser.cs`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/TerminalWaypointFixTests.cs`, `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Data/NavigationDatabaseTerminalWaypointTests.cs` |
| **Split concatenated commands (`DCT X CM 50`)** | `Commands/CommandSchemeParser.cs` (`ExpandMultiCommandGreedy`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ResolveAllFixes`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/ZoaParseFixTests.cs` |
| **Add an argument form to an existing command (parse, canonical text, readback, speech, refusal, docs)** | `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ParseByType`, `ParseHeading`; `ParseFlyHeading` for `FH E`) → `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` (canonical + description) → `Commands/FlightCommandHandler.cs` / `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` → `Pilot/PhraseologyVerbalizer.cs` + `Speech/PhraseologyRules.cs` → `Commands/VfrCommandPolicy.cs` / client `Services/VfrCommandGate.cs` → `COMMANDS.md` → `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` |
| **Add a new command** | `CommandRegistry.cs` (`CommandScheme.cs` is derived from `CommandRegistry.All`: no edit) → `CommandSchemeParser.cs` → `CommandDispatcher.cs` → appropriate `*CommandHandler.cs` (+ `VfrCommandPolicy.cs` if it is a VFR-only verb) → `CommandDescriber.cs` arms → the phases' `CanAcceptCommand` arms (only a verb a phase must answer for; a via or speed command, like `DVIA` or `DVXM`, needs none) → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs` (`IsAviationCommand`) → `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` (the record) + `CompoundPolicy.cs` → `Speech/PhraseologyRules.cs` (a spoken form) → `COMMANDS.md` + `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` (then `node tools/build-cheatsheet.mjs`) → test `ClassifyCommandCompletenessTests` |
| **Add a standalone airborne nav verb that resumes the filed route (`OC`)** | `Commands/CanonicalCommandType.cs` → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` → `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` → `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` → `Commands/FlightCommandHandler.cs` → `LiveTraffic/LiveTrafficAssumer.cs` (`FindRouteRejoin`, `InstallRouteFrom`, `NextFixAhead`) → `Scenarios/ArrivalRouteResolver.cs` → `Phases/Tower/DepartureProcedurePhase.cs`, `InitialClimbPhase.cs` → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs` → tests `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Commands/ProceedOnCourseTests.cs` |
| **Add a speed-until-fix variant of a via or speed command (`DVXM`)** | `Commands/CommandSchemeParser.cs` (`ExpandSpeedUntil`) → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` → `Commands/ApproachCommandParser.cs` (`ParseDvia`, `ParseDvxm`) → `Commands/NavigationCommandHandler.cs` (`DispatchDescendVia`, `DispatchDescendViaExceptSpeed`, `SpeedStandsUnderClearance`) → `Commands/FlightCommandHandler.cs` (`ApplySpeedResume`, `EndExceptSpeed`) → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` → `COMMANDS.md` → `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` → tests `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/DescendViaExceptSpeedTests.cs` |
| **Add a form to an existing command's argument (`SPD RFAS`)** | `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ParseSpeed`) → `CommandRegistry.cs` → `COMMANDS.md` → `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/SpeedParserTests.cs` |
| **TAXI autocomplete / modifier keywords** | [`command-input-ux.md`](command-input-ux.md) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` (TAXI definition) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/CommandDefinition.cs` (`CompoundModifier`) → `src/Yaat.Client/Services/ArgumentSuggester.cs` (`OfferableModifiers`) → tests `ArgumentSuggesterTaxiSlotOneTests`, `ArgumentSuggesterTaxiwayTests` |
| **Change what EAPP / the expected approach stores, or add an expected-approach form (the visual `VIS28R`)** | `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ParseExpectApproach`) → `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` (`ExpectApproachCommand` case) → `Commands/ApproachCommandHandler.cs` (`TryExpectVisualApproach`, `ApproachResolutionContext`, `AutoResolveApproach`) → `Data/Airport/ApproachIds.cs` (`TryParseVisual`, `ExpectingText`: every reader of an approach id asks it before CIFP) → `AircraftApproachState.cs` (`Expected`) → readers `Commands/NavigationCommandHandler.cs` (`DispatchJfac`), `Simulation/SimulationEngine.Generators.cs` (`ResolveArrivalRunway`), `Simulation/ScenarioRunwaySignals.cs`, `Pilot/PilotSayBuilder.cs` (SEAPP), `Pilot/PhraseologyVerbalizer.cs` (readback) → snapshots `Simulation/Snapshots/AircraftApproachStateDto.cs` → yaat-server `DtoConverter.cs` / `AircraftChangeTracker.cs` → client `ContextMenus/MenuCatalog.cs` (`SmartVisualRunway`) |
| **Change CVA (visual approach clearance), how a pilot picks a runway for an airport-level one, or whether a field is towered** | `Commands/ApproachCommandParser.cs` (`ParseCva`) → `Commands/ApproachCommandHandler.cs` (`TryClearedVisualApproach`, `RefuseAirportLevelVisual`, `PilotChosenVisualRunway`) → `ControllerAi/ToweredAirports.cs` (the towered set, from the room's own, roster and neighbour ARTCC configs; `DispatchContext.ToweredAirports`) → `ControllerAi/RunwayInUse.cs` → `Simulation/ActiveRunways.cs` / `SimScenarioState.cs` → `Phases/RunwayLandability.cs` → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` (`FormatCva*`) → recording `Simulation/RecordingArchive.cs` (`towered-airports.json.br`) and yaat-server `Simulation/RoomResourcePin.cs`, `Simulation/TrainingBroadcastService.cs` (`BuildToweredAirports`) |
| **Change the altitude an approach-clearance command (PTAC/CAPP) maintains or records as assigned** | `Commands/ApproachCommandHandler.cs` (`TryPtac`, `ResolvePtacAltitude`, `PtacVerticalDirection`; the CAPP crossing altitude in `TryClearedApproach`) → `Commands/FlightCommandHandler.cs` (CM/DM, `CancelViaClearances`) → `ControlTargets.cs` (`AssignedAltitude`) → `Phases/Tower/FinalApproachPhase.cs` → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Simulation/PtacAssignedAltitudeTests.cs`, `ApproachCommandHandlerTests.cs` → COMMANDS.md |
| **An approach clearance (CAPP/CAPPSI/JAPP/PTAC) or EAPP reads back wrong or not at all** | [`pilot-phraseology.md`](pilot-phraseology.md) → `Commands/ApproachCommandHandler.cs` (`ClearedApproachReadback`, `JoinApproachReadback`, `PtacReadback`: the readback builders, set on `CommandResult.PilotReadback`) → `Pilot/ApproachReadbackName.cs` (type, variant and runway words) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`VerbalizeForReadback`, `BuildApproachClause` for a later block or a handler with no readback) → `Pilot/PhraseologyVerbalizer.cs` (the EAPP render, `CrossFixReadback`) → `Pilot/AviationFloor.cs` (`RunwayClearanceTypes`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Commands/ApproachClearanceReadbackTests.cs` |
| **Change which runway end a pilot, terminal or status line names** | [`pilot-phraseology.md`](pilot-phraseology.md) (AIM-grounded rules) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Data/Airport/RunwayCrossingEnd.cs` (`Spoken`, `HasActiveEnd`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Phases/Ground/RunwayTailClearance.cs` → `src/Yaat.Sim/Pilot/PhraseologyVerbalizer.cs` (`RunwayEndNamed`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Phases/Ground/HoldingShortPhase.cs`, `HoldingInPositionPhase.cs`, `HoldingAfterExitPhase.cs`, `ClearRunwayPhase.cs`, `CrossingRunwayPhase.cs`, `FollowingPhase.cs`, `GroundNavigator.cs` → `src/Yaat.Sim/Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`Build*`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/AircraftStatusDescriber.cs` → `src/Yaat.Sim/Simulation/ActiveRunways.cs` → `src/Yaat.Sim/Phases/PhaseContext.cs` (`ActiveRunways`) |
| **Change what a hold-short phase, status line or CROSS menu text calls a runway** | `src/Yaat.Sim/Phases/Ground/HoldingShortPhase.cs` (`ActiveEndOf`, `_displayEnd` → `Name`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Simulation/Snapshots/PhaseSnapshotDto.cs` (`HoldingShortPhaseDto.DisplayEnd`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/AircraftStatusDescriber.cs` → `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (the hold-short and CROSS results) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Data/Airport/HoldShortAnnotator.cs` (`CombinedName`) and `RunwayCrossingEnd.cs` (`TailOverNamer`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Situation/SituationFlagCalculator.cs` (`NextCrossingRunway`) → `src/Yaat.Sim/ControllerAi/Rules/TaxiRouteProgress.cs` (`CrossingEnd`) and `RunwayCrossingRule.cs` → `src/Yaat.Client.Core/ContextMenus/HoldShortMenuHelper.cs` → `src/Yaat.Client/ViewModels/GroundViewModel.cs` (`HeldTarget.Of`) → yaat-server `DtoConverter.cs` (copies the phase name) |
| **Add an argument/modifier to PTAC/PTACF (turn direction, PH/PA) end to end** | `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` (`PositionTurnAltitudeClearanceCommand`) → `Commands/ApproachCommandParser.cs` (`ParsePtac`) → `Commands/ApproachCommandHandler.cs` (`TryPtac`, `ResolvePtacHeading`, `ApplyPtacTargets`, `PtacReadback`) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`BuildApproachClause`) → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` (`FormatPtacCanonical`/`FormatPtacNatural`) → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` → `Speech/PhraseologyRules.cs` (`PtacRules`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Commands/PtacTurnDirectionTests.cs`, `Simulation/PtacPresentHeadingTests.cs`, `Commands/ApproachClearanceReadbackTests.cs`, `Speech/PhraseologyMapperTests.cs` (`Ptac_Rules`) → COMMANDS.md, `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` |
| **Force a turn direction on a heading command** | `Commands/FlightCommandHandler.cs` (`ApplyTurnLeft`/`ApplyTurnRight`) → `ControlTargets.cs` (`PreferredTurnDirection`) → `FlightPhysics.cs` (`UpdateHeading`); PTAC sets it from an `L`/`R` heading prefix in `ApproachCommandHandler.ApplyPtacTargets` |
| **Add a global typed command like HFR or ARWY** | [`command-pipeline.md`](command-pipeline.md) (One routing table) → `Commands/CanonicalCommandType.cs` → `CommandRegistry.cs` → `CommandParser.cs` (the parse; `ParseCommandList` if its argument may hold commas) → `CommandSchemeParser.cs` → `CompoundPolicy.cs` → `ParsedCommand.cs` (the record) → `Simulation/Actions/ActionArm.cs` (`ArmTable`) / `ActionArms.cs` (the body) → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs` (kind, `ActionScope.Global`) → `CommandDescriber.cs` → `docs/command-cheatsheet.json` → `COMMANDS.md`; tests `ClassifyCommandCompletenessTests.cs`, `ActiveRunwaysCommandParserTests.cs`, `Simulation/ActiveRunwaysCommandTests.cs`, yaat-server `tests/Yaat.Server.Tests/ActiveRunwaysCommandRoutingTests.cs` |
| **A global (room-addressed) verb: client routing and server scope** | [`command-pipeline.md`](command-pipeline.md) (Verbs sent without a selection) → `Yaat.Client/ViewModels/MainViewModel.cs` (`GlobalCommandHandlerFor`, `HandleGlobalCommand`) → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs` (kind, `ScopeOf`, `MalformedGlobal`) → `Simulation/CommandScopes.cs` (`SendsWithoutSelection`, `VerbKinds`) → `Simulation/Actions/ActionArm.cs` / `ActionArms.cs` / `ActionRouter.cs` → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` → tests `Simulation/Actions/ActionRouterTests.cs`, `Simulation/CommandScopesTests.cs`, `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/ViewModels/MainViewModelGlobalCommandTests.cs` |
| **Add a global student radio verb (a controller transmission that names no aircraft, like the unaddressed say again `AGAINU`)** | `Simulation/CommandScopes.cs` (`VerbKinds`) → `Simulation/RecordedCommandClassifier.cs` (kind, `ActionScope.Global`) → `Simulation/Actions/ActionArm.cs` / `ActionArms.cs` (the arm picks the aircraft from snapshotted state, then reuses the addressed verb's dispatch and `ApplyPostDispatch`) → `Yaat.Client/ViewModels/MainViewModel.cs` (`GlobalCommandHandlerFor`; `AsUnaddressedSayAgain` for typed sugar) → [`command-pipeline.md`](command-pipeline.md) (Verbs sent without a selection); tests `Pilot/SayAgainUnaddressedTests.cs`, `Simulation/CommandScopesTests.cs`, `MainViewModelGlobalCommandTests.cs` |
| **A re-TAXI / TAXI response note (what it dropped, implicit crossing, push notes)** | [phases.md](phases.md) (*A re-TAXI drops stored clearances and says so*) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (`TryTaxiCore`, `CaptureDepartureRunwayAssignment`, `WarnIfDepartureRunwayChanged`, `WithResponseNotes`, `DroppedClearancesNote`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Commands/TaxiRunwayChangeTests.cs` |
| **Change how an aircraft rolls into or out of a turn (the ramp, the roll-in times, where a turn starts)** | [`flight-physics.md`](./flight-physics.md) (Heading integration, the turn leads) → `FlightPhysics.cs` (`TurnModel`, `UpdateHeading`, `ApplyRampedTurn`, `ComputeAnticipationDistanceNm`, `ComputeArcBlendedHeading`, `SequencingThreshold`, `FlyByTurnFor`) → `AircraftPerformance.cs` (`TurnRate`) + `AircraftCategory.cs` (`CategoryPerformance.RollInSeconds`) → `TurnShape.cs` (`LeadNm`, `InterceptLeadNm`) → the phases that start a turn (`Phases/Pattern/BasePhase.cs` `TurnLeadNm`, `CrosswindPhase.cs`, `DownwindPhase.cs`, `Phases/Approach/InterceptCoursePhase.cs`, `ApproachNavigationPhase.cs`, `Phases/Tower/FinalApproachPhase.cs`, `DepartureProcedurePhase.cs`) → `NavRouteOverlayProjector.cs` and client `Views/Scope/ScopeHeadingVector.cs` (`TurnGeometry`) → `AircraftState.cs` (`BankAngle`, `AppliedTurnRateDegPerSec`) + `Simulation/Snapshots/AircraftSnapshotDto.cs`; the session setting is `Simulation/SimScenarioState.cs` (`TurnModel`) → [`snapshots-and-replay.md`](./snapshots-and-replay.md) → tests `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/TurnRampTests.cs`, `TurnLeadTests.cs`, yaat-server `tests/Yaat.Server.Tests/TurnModelGateTests.cs` |
| **Change how a taxiing or rolling aircraft's nose steers (the ground steering ramp, its rate ceiling)** | [`ground/navigator.md`](./ground/navigator.md) → `Phases/Ground/GroundNavigator.cs` → `AircraftCategory.cs` (`GroundSteerRampSeconds`, `GroundSteerRateCeilingDegPerSec`) → `FlightPhysics.cs` (`UpdateHeading`, `UpdateGroundSteering`, `ApplyRampedTurn`) → `Phases/Ground/RunwayExitPhase.cs`, `Phases/Tower/LandingPhase.cs`, `Phases/Tower/LineUpPhase.cs` → `AircraftState.cs` (`AppliedTurnRateDegPerSec`) → tests `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Physics/GroundSteeringRampTests.cs`, `TurnRampTests.cs`, `LineUpGeometryTests.cs` |
| **Change where the ramped ground steer aims near a node (look-ahead past a node, pre-turn blend, route walk)** | [`ground/navigator.md`](./ground/navigator.md) (Pure-pursuit steering on straights, [Entry-alignment threshold](./ground/navigator.md#entry-alignment-threshold)) → `Phases/Ground/GroundNavigator.cs` (`TickStraight`, `FinalWindowSteerDeg`, `PastNodeSteerDeg`, `BuildRouteWalkLegs`, `PointAlongRoute`, `BuildSpeedConstraints`) → `Data/Airport/AirportGroundLayout.cs` (`GroundArc.PointAtFt`, `DirectionalEdge`) → `Data/Airport/TaxiRoute.cs` → tests `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Physics/GroundNavigatorSteeringRampTests.cs` |
| **Add an RPO-only advisory note to a command response** | `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (`WithResponseNotes`, `PushBackStandTaxiNote`) → `Commands/CommandDispatcher.cs` (passing `IsScenarioScripted`; `ResultReachesController` says whether the response line reaches the controller, else a note goes to `PendingWarnings`, as in `GroundCommandHandler.PushTurnAboutNote.cs`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiOffStandNoteTests.cs` |
| **Taxi to a free stand by name prefix (`TAXI A @B*`, `TAXIAUTO @SIG*`)** | `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (`ResolveRemarkGate`, `ResolveStandPrefix`) → `Commands/GroundCommandParser.cs` → `Pilot/ArrivalParkingPicker.cs` (`PrefixPool`, `PickFree`) → `Data/AirportSidecarDefinition.cs` (`nonGateParking`) → `COMMANDS.md` → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiToStandPrefixTests.cs` |
| **Read a gate or parking from the flight-plan remarks, or resolve a parking-area remark to a free stand (`@GATE`, datablock)** | `src/Yaat.Sim/FlightPlanParking.cs` → `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (`TryTaxiCore`, `ResolveRemarkGate`; an area-only remark goes on through `ResolveRemarkArea` to a free stand that fits the type) → `Phases/ArrivalExitSide.cs` (`AreaStands`) → `Pilot/ArrivalParkingPicker.cs` (`Choose`, `PickFree`, `PickFreeStandInArea`) → `Data/Airport/StandFit.cs` → `Pilot/PhraseologyVerbalizer.cs` (`RenderTaxiDestinationClause`, reads back `TaxiCommand.DestinationArea` and `TaxiAutoCommand.DestinationArea`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/FlightPlanParkingTests.cs`, `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiToRemarkGateTests.cs` |
| **A full parking area: where the arrival holds and what it reports** | `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.cs` (`ResolveRemarkArea`, no free stand: routes toward the pool's pick with `TaxiCommand.HoldAtAreaRampEdge`) → `Commands/GroundCommandHandler.RampEdgeHold.cs` (`CutAtAreaRampEdge`) → `Data/Airport/TaxiRoute.cs` (`DestinationParkingArea`; `TryReRouteTaxi` keeps it for `RTAXI`) → `Phases/Ground/TaxiingPhase.cs` (`CompleteRoute`; `TryTaxiToFreedStand` rechecks the area once at the hold and continues to a stand freed during the taxi) → `Pilot/ArrivalParkingPicker.cs` (`PickFreeStandInArea`) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`BuildHoldingAtRampNoParking`) → `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiToRemarkGateTests.cs`, `ReRouteTaxiTests.cs` |
| **Add a proactive pilot request (request lower) for an IFR arrival** | [`solo-training-pilot-speech.md`](solo-training-pilot-speech.md) (Pending-request reminders) → `src/Yaat.Sim/Pilot/PilotProactive.cs` (`TickDescentRequest`; `TickArrivalApproachRequest` as the template) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`BuildDescentRequest`, `QueueSoloPilotTransmission`) → `Pilot/PilotRequestTracker.cs` + `Pilot/PilotPendingRequest.cs` (kind, `ResolveResponse`, `IsMoot`) → `Simulation/SimulationEngine.Tick.cs` (`TickPilotProactive`) → tests `Pilot/DescentRequestTests.cs`, `Pilot/PilotResponderTests.cs`, `Simulation/Issue1380ApproachAssignmentRequestTests.cs` |
| **Add a new phase** | `Phase.cs` (base) → new phase class → `PhaseList.cs` (registration) → `PhaseRunner.cs` (lifecycle) → `PhaseSnapshotDto.cs` (serialization) → `CommandDispatcher.cs` (acceptance) |
| **Add a REPORT leg (pattern report)** | `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` (`ReportTrigger`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (REPORT parse) → `Commands/NavigationCommandHandler.cs` (`DispatchReport`) → `AircraftApproachState.cs` (`ReportArmed*` flags) + `Simulation/Snapshots/AircraftApproachStateDto.cs` → `Phases/Pattern/PatternReportHelper.cs` → the leg's phase (`DownwindPhase.cs` for midfield, with its `MidfieldReportIssued` latch and `PhaseSnapshotDto.cs`) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (wilco and report builders) → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs`, `CommandRegistry.cs` → `COMMANDS.md`, `docs/command-cheatsheet.json`; tests `Commands/ReportCommandParseTests.cs`, `Commands/ReportDispatchTests.cs`, `Pilot/ReportMidfieldTests.cs` |
| **Make the pilot report something later (reaching an altitude, at a fix, turning base)** | [`solo-training-pilot-speech.md`](solo-training-pilot-speech.md) → `Commands/ParsedCommand.cs` (`ReportTrigger`, `ReportCommand`) → `Commands/CommandRegistry.cs` (`Report`) → `Commands/CommandParser.cs` (`ParseReport`, `ParseReportCancel`, `IsReportFixName`) → `Commands/CommandDescriber.cs` → `Commands/NavigationCommandHandler.cs` (`DispatchReport`, `DispatchReportCancel`) → `AircraftApproachState.cs` + `Simulation/Snapshots/AircraftApproachStateDto.cs` + `ArmedReportsDto.cs` → `Pilot/PilotProactive.cs` (`TickReportTriggers`) → `Pilot/PilotResponder.cs` (`BuildReportWilcoClause`, `BuildAtFixReport`, `RouteByTunedPosition`) → client `ContextMenus/ReportWhenFlyout.cs` → tests `ReportDispatchTests`, `ReportAltitudeTests`, `ArmedReportsDtoTests`, `SnapshotRoundTripTests` → `COMMANDS.md` REPORT section |
| **Altitude commands** | `AltitudeResolver.cs`, `FlightCommandHandler.cs`, `FlightPhysics.cs` (UpdateAltitude), `ControlTargets.cs`; the altitude handlers leave speed state alone, so an assigned speed survives — see the speed cascade in [`flight-physics.md`](flight-physics.md) |

## Layers

One entry per project: what it owns, what it may reference, and what it must never depend on. The project-reference direction is `Yaat.Client` → `Yaat.Client.Core` → `Yaat.Client.Tdls` → `Yaat.Client.Strips` → `Yaat.Sim`; a type used by both the desktop app and a web front-end goes in Strips or Tdls, never in `Yaat.Client`.

- **`Yaat.Sim`** (`src/Yaat.Sim/`): owns all aviation logic and the simulation: flight physics, phases, commands, the tick spine, snapshots and replay, scenario loading, navdata, pilot and controller AI, the STT rule engine, and the wire and state types the server and clients share.

  References no project in the repo (packages: Geo, Google.Protobuf, Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions at run time, and Grpc.Tools at build time only, `PrivateAssets="All"`); never Avalonia, SkiaSharp, LM-Kit or any client project.
- **`Yaat.Client.Strips`** (`src/Yaat.Client.Strips/`): the vStrips UI (strip bays, find, printing), WASM-clean. References `Yaat.Sim`; never Avalonia.Desktop, Velopack, file IO, `Yaat.Client.Core` or `Yaat.Client`.
- **`Yaat.Client.Tdls`** (`src/Yaat.Client.Tdls/`): the vTDLS UI, WASM-clean. References `Yaat.Sim` and `Yaat.Client.Strips`; never the desktop-only projects.
- **`Yaat.Client.Core`** (`src/Yaat.Client.Core/`): the LM-Kit-free desktop client core: `ServerConnection` (SignalR and DTOs), `UserPreferences`, `AppLog`, `ClientVersionGate`, `UpdateService`, CRC alias parsing, the shared context-menu catalog, `ConnectViewModel`. References `Yaat.Sim`, `Yaat.Client.Strips` and `Yaat.Client.Tdls` (packages include Avalonia.Desktop and Velopack); never `Yaat.Client` or LM-Kit.
- **`Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis`** (`src/Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis/`): raw mouse input through a window's own platform input callback. References no project in the repo; only `Yaat.Client` references it.
- **`Yaat.Client`** (`src/Yaat.Client/`): the Avalonia desktop app: `MainViewModel`, radar and ground rendering, the instructor's windows, the automation pipe, speech. The sole owner of LM-Kit.NET. References `Yaat.Client.Core`, `Yaat.Client.InputSynthesis` and `Yaat.Sim`; nothing references it but tests, `tools/Yaat.SpeechSandbox` and yaat-server's GuideCapture and client-driver tests.
- **`tools/Yaat.VStrips.Web`, `tools/Yaat.VTdls.Web`**: the Avalonia Browser (WASM) front-ends over Strips, and over Tdls with Strips. Never `Yaat.Client.Core` or `Yaat.Client`. yaat-server builds and hosts them from `src/Yaat.Server/Dockerfile` through `extern/yaat/`, which is why `wasm-tools` is a build prerequisite.
- **Other tools**: `tools/Yaat.LayoutInspector`, `tools/Yaat.CifpInspector` and `tools/Yaat.PrecomputeCache` reference `Yaat.Sim` only; `tools/Yaat.SpeechSandbox` references `Yaat.Client`. `tools/Yaat.RecordingConsolidator` (dedupes the test-data recordings; the `consolidate-recordings` skill) and `tools/Yaat.Scratch` (a bare project for throwaway experiments) reference no project.
- **Tests**: `Yaat.Sim.Tests` references `Yaat.Sim`; `Yaat.Client.Tests` references `Yaat.Client`, `Yaat.Client.Core` and `Yaat.SpeechSandbox`; `Yaat.Client.UI.Tests` references `Yaat.Client`, `Yaat.Client.Core` and `Yaat.Sim`; `Yaat.Client.UI.Render.Tests` references `Yaat.Client`; `Yaat.LayoutInspector.Tests` and `Yaat.PrecomputeCache.Tests` reference their tool.
- **yaat-server** (sibling repo): `Yaat.Server` references `Yaat.Sim` as a project reference; of its tools only GuideCapture references `Yaat.Client`, the client-driver MCP compiles yaat's `src/Yaat.Client/Automation/Protocol/*.cs` as linked source, and the MCP's tests reference `Yaat.Client`, `Yaat.Client.Core` and `Yaat.Sim`; yaat references no yaat-server project (see the footgun below). Its own Task Index, layers and file maps are in `docs/architecture.md` of the yaat-server repo.

## Integration Footguns

- **Modify `AircraftState`** → must mirror changes in `AircraftSnapshotDto.cs` + add migration in `SnapshotSchemaMigrator.cs`
- **New command type** → must add to `CanonicalCommandType` enum, `CommandRegistry` definitions, AND the verb's parse (`CommandParser.cs` switch). `CommandScheme.Default()` is derived from `CommandRegistry.All`, so it needs no edit. Tests enforce completeness.
- **New phase** → must add `[JsonDerivedType]` attribute in `PhaseSnapshotDto.cs` for serialization
- **Modify `ControlTargets`** → check `ControlTargetsDto.cs` snapshot parity
- **Aircraft performance** → sync `AircraftProfiles.json` + `AircraftProfileDatabase` + `AircraftPerformance.cs` fallback logic; per-type corrections go in `AircraftProfileOverrides.json` (see `docs/aircraft-performance.md`)
- **New hub call that replaces or clears a room's scenario** → refuse it while `TrainingRoom.LoadingBy` is set, before the tick gate and again inside it (`TrainingHub.ThrowIfLoadingScenario` / `UnlessLoadingScenario`), and gate its client command on `MainViewModel.IsRoomLoading` (see `docs/server-rooms-and-hub.md` § Scenario load)
- **New reload or reconstruction path** → read the room's `ResourcePin` (ARTCC configs and layouts), never `ArtccConfigService` / `AirportGroundDataService` directly, or a rewind stops reproducing the live run after vNAS changes a map or config
- **yaat compiles no yaat-server project** → `yaat.slnx` names none and no yaat project references one (GuideCapture, which hosts the server in-process, is a yaat-server tool). A yaat build, prek's pre-push `dotnet build` included, therefore does not compile yaat-server: a `Yaat.Sim` change that breaks it is caught by `tools/test-all.ps1` and the yaat-server step of yaat CI, not by the push hook. The two WASM projects' post-publish `wwwroot` copy is a file copy, not a compile edge.
- **New scenario-JSON field that names an airport or ARTCC** → add it to `ScenarioResourceManifest` as well as the loader, or the server's load never prefetches it (`ScenarioResourceManifestTests.Corpus_LoaderAsksForNoAirportOutsideTheManifest` catches the airport half)
- **yaat files that also compile in yaat-server** → `src/Yaat.Client/Automation/Protocol/*.cs`, `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/Helpers/{AutomationHostFixture,AutomationPipeTestClient,InteractiveDesktop}.cs` and `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/ModuleInit.cs` are compiled by yaat-server's client-driver MCP and its tests. A change to any of them can break yaat-server's build alone; yaat's prek does not catch it, `tools/test-all.ps1` and the yaat-server build step of yaat CI do.

  The reverse also holds: every yaat-server build builds `Yaat.Client`, `Yaat.Client.Core`, Strips and Tdls into yaat's own `bin/` and `obj/`, so a running `Yaat.Client` locks DLLs a yaat-server commit needs and concurrent yaat and yaat-server builds share those folders. See yaat-server `docs/client-driver-mcp.md`.

## Test locations

> Before adding a test, open [`docs/test-map.md`](./test-map.md): it maps every *class* of test (shape → folder → what it pins → harness → placement rule) by subsystem, so you can tell whether a behaviour is already pinned and where the new test belongs. The list below is the flagship-file index; the map is the almanac.

- **Sim tests**: `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/` — commands, phases, physics, parsers, nav data
  - **Collection order**: `CollectionWeightOrderer.cs` (assembly-level xunit collection orderer: weight descending from the embedded `collection-weights.json`, ties and unknown collections by key, ordinal) and `CollectionWeightOrdererTests.cs`
  - **Approach readback variants**: `Commands/ApproachClearanceVariantTests.cs` (the CAPP/CAPPSI/JAPP/JAPPSI/EAPP spoken rows by band, the later-block bare CAPP naming the expected approach), `Commands/PtacVariantTests.cs` (the PTAC/PTACF spoken rows by band, direction and vertical verb, and the cases that stay canonical) and `Commands/VisualApproachAndFollowVariantTests.cs` (the runway and airport-level CVA and the airborne FOLLOW rows by band, Terminal and RPO texts canonical).

    `Pilot/ChainedReadbackBandTests.cs` (a readback of two or more commands is never chatty: quiet speaks the normal words, busy keeps terse rows on every clause), `Pilot/LastTransmissionTests.cs` (the last-line store written at dequeue, a repeat copy clearing the pending repeat, snapshot round trip and omission while empty), `Pilot/SayAgainTests.cs` (the say-again drain: the last line or the callsign alone or "I didn't say anything" behind the pilot's own line, one copy per request, the request dropped when the aircraft leaves the frequency, no student bookkeeping) and `Pilot/SayAgainReplayTests.cs` (a rewind that restores the pending flag still owes the repeat), `Commands/SayAgainParseTests.cs` (`AGAIN` / `AGAIN CS` parsing)
  - **Filed speed text**: `AircraftFlightPlanSpeedFieldTests.cs` (`FormatSpeedField`: SC wins over a Mach number, Mach is M plus three digits of hundredths, knots are bare digits, no speed is empty)
  - **Pathfinding**: `Pathfinding/FilletCornerRoutingTests.cs` (fillet arc vs square-pivot routing), `Pathfinding/SfoDetourHonorsNamedTaxiwayTests.cs` (gate G3 `TAXI A Q B F 28L HS 1L`: the parking-node A→Q bridge must still reach A — pins the honour-named-taxiway behaviour any detour re-ranking has to keep), `Pathfinding/AutoRouterPruningTests.cs` (A* pruning via arrival taxiway), `Pathfinding/ArcRadiusFloorAdmissibilityTests.cs` (steerable radius floor)
  - **Fillet arc speed**: `Fillet/GroundArcSpeedProfileTests.cs` (local-curvature arc speed). `GroundNavigatorArcSpeedProfileTests.cs` (navigator speed profile application). `GroundNavigatorStraightHandoffTests.cs` (straight-after-arc heading release).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/NodeAimedEntryOntoFilletTests.cs` (a TAXI issued inside the turning diameter of a fillet's near end, facing away from it — SFO gate E2 SKW5707, `5d33df162626.zip` — hands the entry-alignment arc over on the straight line to the fillet's far end instead of its Bézier, never teleports, round-trips a mid-line snapshot, and is not held to the fillet's cornering speed since the straight does not corner). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakPushedNearReversalOpeningTests.cs` (a B38M just pushed off OAK stand 4 and nearly reversed from `TAXI TC U W W1`: the opening arc is aimed past the short RAMP straight, onto the line to the next fillet's end, and the fillet starts on its curve without a teleport).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/AimedPastOntoHoldShortBarTests.cs` (an entry-alignment arc aimed past onto a hold-short junction node retires the leg and re-aims the navigator's internally-moved target onto the junction's painted bar, not the junction itself, bypassing `TaxiingPhase`'s own segment set-up)
  - **Ground taxi**: `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakUwFilletCornerTests.cs` (bundle replay: fillet routing + speed profiles). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/HoldOnCurveTests.cs` (a `HOLD` landing mid-fillet keeps the navigator ticking, so `RES` does not write the aircraft backwards onto the stale playback pose — the hold contract in [`ground/navigator.md`](./ground/navigator.md)).

    `GroundNavigatorRouteEndSpeedTests.cs` (`RouteEndSpeedKts`: a taxi whose destination-runway bar is already covered by a stored clearance arrives rolling, an uncleared one still arrives stopped; plus the two runway-incursion guards — a heavy reaches 0 kt before the roll per 7110.65 §3-9-6.c, and a `CTOC` inside the bar does not cross it).

    `Simulation/SfoCtoIntersectionDepartureE2ETests.cs` (the SFO taxiway-E intersection departure reaches the takeoff roll within the pinned seconds of the clearance and never parks on the runway side of the holding position).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakSimplePushThenTaxiApproachTests.cs` (unit-level: a PUSH-then-TAXI route starting behind the aircraft is driven to via a free-space leg, not teleported onto). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakSimplePushThenTaxiApproachReplayTests.cs` (E2E replay of the same PUSH/TAXI session off a recording).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakPushTeThenTaxiTests.cs` (YAAT-708: a B737/B738 held on KOAK TE after a bare `PUSH TE` off gate 31 or 29 is accepted `TAXI T U W RWY 30`, `TAXI $E` and `TAXI TE $E`, turning the 117°/104° corner toward T; the taxi to spot E flies the corner before the spot stop).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/PushThenTaxiSweepTests.cs` (YAAT-41: for each KOAK/KSFO stand and each taxilane its push menu offers, a B738 pushed by a bare `PUSH {lane}` is accepted a `TAXI` onto every taxiway the lane meets, and moves off or completes its route; the full sweep is `Nightly`, the known OAK/SFO cases run on every change).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiApproachLegTests.cs` (`TaxiApproachLeg.Prepend` unit cover: the four refusal shapes plus the along-runway-roll case, and the runway-bar start measured along the occupied edge). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiFromShortOfARunwayBarTests.cs` (a TAXI from short of a runway bar gets an approach leg to the bar, and holds at it when the aircraft is within its painted stop). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiFromAFilletArcToARunwayBarTests.cs` (the same for an aircraft on the fillet arc ending at the bar: approach leg to the bar, nose stopped at the marking). `VirtualNodeTests.cs` (position-hashed virtual-node ids: same position → same id, a foot apart → a different one, cross-process determinism).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakTaxiMidRunwayCrossingTests.cs` (YAAT-710: a `TAXI` given to a C56X crossing KOAK 28R, on the runway between its bars or off its pavement short of the north marking, keeps 28R cleared and finishes clearing it instead of holding at the far bar).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SeaCrossingNotYetReachedTests.cs` (YAAT-732: a C172 on KSEA C past the line through D's 34R marking but short of the crossing is held by a plain HOLD and holds short as written on a re-route that leaves the crossing out). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/OakTaxiToOccupiedStandTests.cs` (YAAT-711: a `TAXI` to a KOAK stand another aircraft is parked on names the occupant in its response, also on the landing rollout, and says nothing for a free stand).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiStartsOnOccupiedTaxiwayTests.cs` (a TAXI to an aircraft mid-way along a straight taxi edge starts on that taxiway, at KOAK C and KSFO B, and one in a fillet arc starts at the arc's end ahead).

    Its route-aware-start cases: a route behind turns a C172 about to the far node, reported `TaxiTurnAboutShape.FromFarEnd` with that node as target until that leg is flown; a route drawn back through a runway holding position, with no leg to it, and a scripted B738 whose node ahead resolves none also report `FromFarEnd`.

    Its gear-fit cases: a B738, lined up or angled 30° across (a reversal of about 150°), refuses "no room to turn around", as do a C25A and an AT76, while a C208 turns about; a B738 angled 60° across (a corner of about 120°) is accepted and turns toward the far node; the angled B738 on a scripted TAXI keeps the route ahead; a scripted TAXI is never refused, and a controller's TAXI after a push (held after a pushback) is accepted too, also across a mid-push snapshot restore.

    Its turn-about cases: a C172 and a C208 cleared to a taxiway behind them on KOAK C, and a C172 on a drawn route to the C node behind, turn about with their centre within the 12.5 ft TDG 1A half-width of C's centreline, at no more than ω·R on their type's turn-about radius (`TurnAboutFit`).

    `Phases/Ground/TurnAboutFitTests.cs` (the gear fit over real FAA records: a C208 and a C172 fit, an E55P, AT76, C25A, B738 and DH8D do not; the C208's 5.85 ft radius; a type absent from the database fits only as a piston or helicopter; with gear figures missing only a TDG 1A non-jet fits; an unrecognised TDG is judged by category).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiwayTurnAboutJunctionTests.cs` (a C172 meeting a reversal of 135° or more at a real KOAK/KSFO taxiway junction, arriving at the node or at a tangent point short of it, keeps rounding the corner at its corner speed instead of turning about at the pivot speed).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiwayTurnAboutAimTests.cs` (where a taxiway turn about's reversal rolls out: N152SP's replay on KOAK D rolls out on D's bearing, holds it to abeam node 366 and turns onto H tangent to H's centreline; a C208, a longer straight and a type with no FAA record re-centre; a C172 at a real acute branch is re-aimed past the junction, not when it holds short there).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TurnAboutAtRunwayBarFootprintTests.cs` (an aircraft at rest short of a KOAK/KSFO runway bar and cleared back over its taxiway turns about with no part past the hold line, else holds, says "unable, no room to turn around on {twy}, holding short of runway {rwy}" once and survives a snapshot; owns `TickOneSecondBySubTick`, the sub-tick stepper mirroring `SimulationEngine.RunSecond`).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiwayDepartureHeadingTests.cs` (`TaxiCoverageRunner.TaxiwayDepartureHeading` faces away from the runway at a bar).
    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/BareTaxiFirstStepHeadingTests.cs` (a bare `TAXI` naming only the taxiway the aircraft is already on is refused and the aircraft stays put: on KOAK B at the south 28L bar, facing either way or holding short of 28L; a directed `TAXI` or one with `HS`/`CROSS` is not bare). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/TaxiReadbackCutTests.cs` (the TAXI readback is cut at the first stopping hold short; a covered `CROSS`/`RES` in the block adds no clause; the continuation readback of a `CROSS`/`RES` mid-route).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoSpot10JoinATests.cs` (YAAT-717: SFO spot 10 `TAXI A` takes no T9, its first segment and every junction stay within the turn limit, and three recorded poses hold; with `Issue396GateBridgeDeepeningTests` it covers the start bridge).

    Its stop and restore cases: N152SP stops square to a bar at node 366, also when the hold short is issued on the held straight, and a restore mid-reversal, on the held straight, mid node turn or on the square stop line replays the uninterrupted run.

    `Helpers/AirTaxiStands.cs` (the stands `Commands/AirTaxiRunwayCrossingWarningTests.cs` sends a helicopter to, to assert the per-runway ATXI crossing warning on real layouts).

    `Helpers/KoakTaxiwayC.cs` (KOAK's long straight C edge west of H, `LongEdgeWestOfH`: the mid-edge pose the turn-about and mid-edge taxi tests stand on).

    `Helpers/RunwayBarFixture.cs` (`RunwayBar(airport, designator)`: a real airport's runway with its TestData ground layout and the lowest-numbered hold-short bar abeam the pavement, null when unavailable; the fouling tests place aircraft against it).

    `Phases/CrossingRunwayTailClearTests.cs` (the tail-clearance leg past a runway crossing follows the aircraft's own route first and the layout's straightest continuation only once the route runs out, and the phase hands the route back at the right segment; a type missing from the FAA database clears by half its CWT fallback length).

    `Commands/GroundExpediteCommandTests.cs` (the `EXP` token on `TAXI`/`TAXIAUTO`/`CROSS`: parsing, the bare-`CROSS EXP` and no-runway refusal, readbacks and echoes, what ends a route or crossing expedite).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/GroundExpediteTaxiTests.cs` (the expedited taxi and crossing physics: cap, acceleration, per-crossing scope, Super/Heavy and `SPD`).

    `Simulation/GroundExpediteRolloutTests.cs` (`TAXI`/`TAXIAUTO … EXP` on the landing rollout: exit choice at the expedite limit, a standing `EXIT x EXP`).

    `Phases/ClearRunwayPhaseFallbackLengthTests.cs` (`CLRWY`'s pull-forward past the hold bar uses the same CWT fallback length for an unknown type). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoVideoRoutePinTests.cs` (the SFO ground-controller routes from the ZOA familiarization video and SOP — 28/1, 28/28, 19/10, 19/19, 10/10, supers — resolve with exactly the hold-shorts each clearance implies; start points by gate/spot/bar/junction name).

    `Helpers/RecordingGroundData.cs` (the ground data a recording test builds its engine on: `Evergreen(why)` serves today's `TestData` layouts, `VersionPinned(archivePath, why)` the archive's bundled ones, `why` required), `Simulation/RecordingArchiveLayoutGuardTests.cs` (fails a test file that opens a layout-carrying archive without one of the two factories, reads `ReadLayout`/`ReadAllLayouts` directly, or builds `new TestAirportGroundData()` for it).

    `Helpers/SubTickDriver.cs` (`TickSubTicksUntil`: steps the spine a sub-tick at a time, calling back after each) and `Helpers/SpeedLossWatch.cs` (the largest per-sub-tick speed loss, leaving out only a stop's final take from ≤ 3 kt to 0; ground stop tests).
    `Helpers/SfoGroundHarness.cs` (engine + SFO layout builder, name-resolved spawns at parking/spot/hold-short/junction, `TickUntil`, order-preserving hold-short assertion with route dump, `DeadlockGuard` for multi-aircraft choreography).

    `Simulation/SfoBravoToOneLeftConnectorTests.cs` (`TAXI A F1 B 1L` / `C Z B 1L` thread the M1 stub to the 1L bar with the `via M1` note, the engine drives it to the hold; 1R / 28L off B still refuse with the same message; the connector finder's accept/reject cases).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoSimultaneousAlleyPushTests.cs` (two tugs pushing into adjacent alley lanes — SFO 5A/5B, SOP 3-5.c.i — both complete with at most a brief in-line yield; a pusher aimed dead at another aircraft still stops while an abeam one clears). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoDepartureFunnelTests.cs` (the 28/28 west-plan departure funnel: four B738s stage at F1-on-1R, A1 and the 1R bar, release 3-1-4-2 with RES/FOLLOWG/CROSS, end in a 28L line ranked 1..4 with nobody on 28L).

    Its `FOLLOWG` release, `Release_3_1_4_2_FollowG`, runs with `GroundConflictDetector` resolving follower-against-other pairs.

    `Phases/Ground/FollowingPhaseHoldShortTests.cs` (a follower reaching its own destination bar holds as a departure, and does not stop at the far-side bar of a runway it is leaving).

    `Pathfinding/ForcedPushLegPlannerTests.cs` (`/PUSH`/`/PULL` legs: only the forced kind after the stand push-off, a `/PUSH` onto a spot ends on the stop, a forced leg with no flyable plan is refused, a pull whose 30 ft tug lead ends on a taxiway is refused, marked points plan onto the pose or are refused on a taxiway, runway or holding position).

    `Data/Airport/TowerCabVideoMapTests.cs` (`TowerCabVideoMap.Read`: which features become two-point segments, file order, the hash, the warnings and the non-FeatureCollection throw; the OAK fixture `TestData/TowerCabMaps/oak.geojson`).
    `Data/Airport/TestAirportGroundDataVideoMapTests.cs` (the shared harness builds OAK on that videomap, so its hold-short points equal the snapped parse and differ from the null-map one).
    `Data/Airport/SpotEdgeBuildTests.cs` (the layout build's spot-edge chains: point resolvers, edge names, skipped entries), `Data/Airport/SpotLinkTests.cs` (the ratchet: every committed layout's spots have an edge, the unlinked ones listed; `Mia_ShippedSpotEdges_LoadAndBuildEveryChain` builds MIA's shipped chains on an unfilleted layout),
    `Data/Airport/StandLeadInTests.cs` (the sidecar `standLeadIns` loader checks and the layout build's lead-in connector, warnings and fall-backs), `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoGateD12LeadInFromATests.cs` (SFO D12 joins A, so `TAXI K A @D12` avoids spot 6B), `Helpers/TestStandLeadIns.cs` (an airport's shipped lead-ins for a test that builds its layout).
    `Data/Airport/HoldShortBarFlagTests.cs` (the layout build marks every runway bar `GroundNode.IsShortBar`, as the cross-track measurement reads it, and no other node).
    `Commands/ParkedStandDepartureTests.cs` (a parked aircraft's stand departure: the layout's answer, the sidecar per-name override and area rule, a helipad as TaxiOut, none off a stand).
    `Commands/PushLegKindAndFreePoseParseTests.cs` (the suffix and `~lat/lon[/facing]` grammar, examples and non-examples, NaN/Infinity refused). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/MarkedPointPushTests.cs` (a marked-point tow flown through the handler ends on the point; a snapshot mid-tow restores to the same end pose and keeps `KeepsItsPlan`; a mid-tow `FACE` is refused as keeping its plan).

    `Phases/Ground/PushbackMoveBoundaryTests.cs` (SFO E6 `PUSH $6B`, a three-move plan: the tug holds 5 kt through the straight→turn boundary, still stops and dwells before the reversal pull, completes on 6B, and a taxi clearance that cuts a continuing move stops the aircraft dead).

    In the same plan every second's speed change stays within `CategoryPerformance.TugAccelRate`/`TugDecelRate`, the tug holds push speed through the turn instead of easing onto a wingtip cap ahead of it (`HoldsPushSpeedUntilTheTurnBrake`), and every stop is reached at the `FinalApproachKts` crawl first.

    `Pathfinding/TugAlleyClearanceTests.cs` (a push kept off the movement area — a spot, stand or node goal — stays outside a taxiway's Airplane Design Group object-free half-width plus a 5 ft margin: SFO taxiway A is ADG IV at 121.5 ft off a 218 ft parallel to Y).
    `Pathfinding/TugOutlineClearanceTests.cs` (`TugOutlineClearance.Clearance` equals `GroundOutline.Clearance` to the bit over pairs of outlines that overlap and that stand clear).

    The airport's own runway-width bucket shares `AirplaneDesignGroups.FromRunwayWidth`'s buckets with the hold-short wingtip floor, F8→7A and E12→7B stay clear via the straight-then-line push, and D7 `PUSH A F1` — sent onto A — is not held to it.

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/PushTowSpeedTests.cs` (SFO E1 `PUSH $7` flown end to end through `PushbackPhase` and physics: the tug holds `PushbackSpeed` through turns while the main gear slows by the cosine of the nose-gear steer angle, a creep move only slows to `PushbackAlignSpeed` over its last `AlignCreepFt` = 30 ft, and the final pull onto the lane turns onto the lane heading without passing it).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/PushReadbackNotesTests.cs` (a `TugPlan`'s warnings — a long push, a far facing junction, a taxiway foul — reach the RPO as parentheticals on the push/`PUSHM`/`FACE`-amendment readback and never the pilot's spoken readback, which is verbalized from the command).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoSixAlleyChoreographyTests.cs` (pusher from D15 into 6A/6B vs an arrival taxiing the other alley lane: never slowed on the far lane to D16; on T6A to gate E9 a moving pusher abreast at ~140 ft costs a bounded hold via the trail limit and a parked one costs nothing — a D-pier push into T6B never enters T6A).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoYankeePushTests.cs` (B12: plain `PUSH Y` ends on Y with the nose on the stand heading; a B752 `PUSH A` control).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/Issue899DetourConnectorTests.cs` (a taxi detour crosses an uncleared runway only straight and never runs along or turns on it, and between two named taxiways uses only digit-named stubs, RAMP, non-movement taxilanes or taxiways the clearance names; the rest is refused "Unable, A and B do not connect" or "no route from A onto B ahead" and the aircraft stays put).

    KCOS `TAXI A2 C2` is refused, C2 being a high-speed exit, unless a sidecar `crossingPairs` entry declares the pair, which the tests declare in themselves over the real layout, and then a B738 is refused "can't make the turn" while a C172 crosses.

    The search and the post-hoc filter agree on every bridge.

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoYankeeTaxiOutPinTests.cs` and `Pathfinding/SfoYankeeConnectorChoiceTests.cs` (after `PUSH Y A1`, `TAXI Y A A1 1R` leaves Y through AY3 ahead of the nose, not AY2 behind it: the connector detour ranks candidate bridges by pavement cost plus a reversal charge against the aircraft's pose — the resolver-level pin and the flown taxi-out).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoSixAlleyArrivalAheadOfPushTests.cs` (an E6 push to 6B against an arrival taxiing T6A to E9: the arrival is through the alley and parked while the push is still on its first leg, the push completes to 6B afterwards, and the two never close inside 90 ft — the former F-6 wedge pin, now a regression guard against a re-introduced hold).

    `Simulation/Ual58Spot9ReversalTests.cs` (SFO bundle replay of the "UAL58 did a loop" report: after `TAXI A F 28L` off spot 9's T9/A junction, the entry-alignment reversal and the turn onto A must cancel into a net ≤180°/peak ≤210° rotation that gets established on A's 27.6° bearing rather than compound into one ~270° sweep; a second case pins the free-space arc leaving gate G10 against overshooting the 21 ft ramp leg before it settles on spot 9).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/ForcedPushTests.cs` (`PUSHF`/`PUSHMF`: parses and describes back to itself, keeps every part of the plain push/tug-move grammar).

    A plain push a parked neighbour or an unnamed taxiway would refuse suggests the forced form, which the neighbour-ranking step keeps the most room to (`TugNeighbourClearance.Shortlist`) and the detector never brakes for. Forcing keeps the runway, holding-position, sanity-guard and start-overlap refusals — with no suggestion — and every `PUSHM` pass-through hint.

    `AircraftGroundOps.ForcedTowIgnoresParked` is set on install and a forced/plain amendment, cleared on completion or a command that clears the tow, and round-trips a snapshot. Each rule a forced plan overrides is noted once on the readback (`TugPlanBuilder.AddOverride` keeps a neighbour at its closest pass).

    A faced goal no template lines up on takes the geometric fallback onto the spot. `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoPushOntoATests.cs` (SFO D11, E13T, E13K, F10, C10, C11: a bare `PUSH A` takes the documented Across rule and ends with the aircraft's centre on A on the stand heading; `PUSHF A` installs the identical plan since nothing is about to force past).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/SfoPushOntoT7AFromF5Tests.cs` (SFO F5 `PUSH T7A`, whose push lands on T7A's filleted end: accepted, read back as across, ends on the stand heading past T7A's end).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/PushmHintE2ETests.cs` (`PUSHM`'s pass-through hints flown end to end on the real SFO ramp beyond the F8 reference case: a hint no arrival passes taken through on a move of its own, and a hint that is a graph node rather than a spot).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/FollowGroundBarStopTests.cs` (the KOAK H1 clip: a `FOLLOWG` follower brakes into the runway 33 bar on C at the piston taxi brake rate, rests with its nose at the hold line, and reports holding short; plus an SFO 28R / D scene on `SfoGroundHarness`, where a B77W follower with its tail inside the exit bar turns back across 28R and stops at that bar).

    The same file covers the graph follow at bars: a follower far behind stays on the taxiways and stops at bar 518; one that exited 33 and turns back toward it stops at its bar, and drops the runway by position; closing on the bar behind a slower lead it brakes at the bar's rate; two B738s in trail keep the nose-to-tail stop gap along the path.

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/FollowGroundOnGraphTests.cs` (KOAK: a follower gives way short of a merge its lead has not passed until the lead's tail clears it, joins behind a lead on its own edge without giving way, turning about first when it faces away, drives on from mid-edge, and holds in position with no plan at runtime).

    Its snapshot cases round-trip the follow route, merge index, lead edge into the merge, give-way, unjoinable latch, navigator and clearing attempts.

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/ClearedRunwayBarArrivalTests.cs` (a pre-cleared runway crossing hands off to `CrossingRunwayPhase` at the bar, not up to the loose arrival threshold short of it).
    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/ReTaxiArmsUnmakeableBarTests.cs` (a re-`TAXI` to a rolling aircraft flags a taxiway or spot bar inside the firm stopping distance unable at arming, silently; a runway bar keeps the dead stop; a bar placed where the aircraft stands is skipped).
    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/GiveWayStopBrakingTests.cs` (the KOAK H2 clip: a `GIVEWAY` keeps taxiing and brakes at the taxi rate to a stop with its centre clear of the crossing track by wingtip clearance, the traffic crosses unstopped and the hold releases; told inside its taxi-rate stopping distance it brakes at no more than the max-effort rate and never passes the point; a `GIVEWAY` past a cleared bar's hold line never speeds up once the crossing stop curve binds, and a crosser meeting head-on traffic past the far bar stops tail-clear of the runway).

    `GiveWayAutoReleaseTests.cs` and `GiveWayInTrailReleaseTests.cs` (the GIVEWAY release at a shared node: held until the traffic's tail clears it, no 1,500 ft cap, no timeout or stationary-bypass release, mutual give-way releasing the lower callsign, a rejoin in trail not a crossing) and `Simulation/SfoGiveWayCrossingTests.cs` (the SFO 28RT recording: UAL2237's GIVEWAY to UAL926 crossing toward F, about 5,000 ft away, holds it short of the node until UAL926's tail has cleared it).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/GroundNavigatorArcRestoreTests.cs` (a snapshot taken mid entry-alignment turn restores onto the original's positions every second; a saved from-node that does not match the segment drops the playback with a warning; a taxiway turn about restored mid-jog and mid-reversal stays on the original's positions).

    `Simulation/GroundTaxi/CrossingRunwayRestoreTests.cs` (a crossing restored mid-slice, or snapshotted again before its first tick, stays on the original's slice segment and path). `Simulation/GroundTaxi/KoakFollowClip.cs` (helper: loads the KOAK clip scenario and replays a `script.txt` one sim-second at a time)
  - **Commands**: `Commands/TrackResolverTests.cs` (the one TCP→TrackOwner chain against the real ZOA config — including the STARS interfacility handoff-code leg — and `ResolveIdentity` precedence: AS override → AI connection → selection → student; the position-callsign and `callsign@tcp` fallbacks; `PositionSelections` snapshot/restore).

  - **Discord Rich Presence**: `DiscordIpcFrameTests.cs` (frame round-trip, the little-endian header, oversize / negative length refused, a peer closing mid-frame).

    `DiscordRichPresenceServiceTests.cs` (the worker against a scripted Discord over an in-memory duplex stream and a manual clock: handshake then `SET_ACTIVITY`, latest-wins, clear closes the connection, back-off while Discord is absent, ping → pong, reconnect after a server close, prompt `Dispose` under a hanging connect, handshake timeout, a clear during the handshake, the 128-char clamp)
  - **Speech telemetry**: `SpeechTelemetryUploaderTests.cs` (each pending sample POSTed oldest first and its marker cleared; a server or transport failure stops the pass and leaves samples pending; a rejected sample is dropped and the rest upload; telemetry off, no server URL, no token, or a sample captured without queueForUpload makes no request).

    `SpeechSampleStoreTests.cs` (upload-pending markers, WriteBundle). `TelemetryTranscriptRegressionTests.cs` (every promoted transcript in `TestData/speech-transcripts/telemetry-regressions.json` maps to its canonical, and the harness fails when a label is wrong)
  - **Bug report filing**: `BugReportIssueBuilderTests.cs` (`BuildUrl`: GitHub new-issue query, title/body round-trip special characters, body carries form + bundle name + environment, not-in-a-room and no-attachment wording, callsigns and expected sections, over-long text truncated to the URL ceiling with a marker and never through a multi-byte escape)
  - **Speech ouroboros**: `AtcOuroborosTests.cs` (every `SynthTemplates` template verifies through the rule mapper or is a known gap, unique keys, three variants per family, deterministic per-seed sampling, unverifiable templates reported and never sampled, per-family/template/total aggregation, baseline diff classification and exit codes), `EvalRunnerSessionStubTests.cs` (an expected.json stub built from a store-written session carries canonical, transcript and callsigns)
- **UI tests**: `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/` — headless window tests for views and layout. `Views/MessageBoxDialogTests.cs` drives a custom and a standard MsBox dialog to a button click so a MessageBox.Avalonia pin that does not load against the shipped Avalonia (the 3.x line under Avalonia 12, GitHub #437) fails there instead of in a user's dialog. `Views/ClearedForTakeoffFlyoutTests.cs` drives the Cleared for takeoff flyout's boxes and rows (VFR and IFR) against the commands they send.

  `Views/ScopeMenuCueTests.cs` (the host's cue and highlight reach the open Scope, its canvas places the cue against the frame's tracks and repaints on a change).

  `Automation/AutomationWaitUntilTests.cs` (the pipe's `wait_until` on a stub `IAutomationState`: each condition kind holds and reports its last value, `mode: all`, a timeout answers met=false rather than an error, an absent callsign reads `absent`).
  `Automation/AutomationPointerTests.cs` (`SyntheticMouse` hover raises PointerEntered once and sets IsPointerOver on a context-menu item; a drag captures on the press, delivers every held move through the capture, releases at the target and loses the capture once; the settings quick-commands drag reorders a row; the ground runway menu hover previews the route; the pipe `hover`/`drag` answer the element type, the site and the steps, a selector hover lands on the element centre, a hover outside the client area is OUT_OF_BOUNDS, a hover in a disabled window is ELEMENT_DISABLED, a hover during a drag is UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION, and bad params are INVALID_PARAM naming the param; the tests whose input must reach a shown window skip without an interactive desktop, through the linked `InteractiveDesktop.cs` probe).

  `log_matches` sees only lines added after the call (also across a log trim) and skips `IsHistory` lines, bad kinds, regexes and rates are `INVALID_PARAM`, the screenshot is taken at the matching poll, `then` actions run in order on a match and not on a timeout
  - **Discord Rich Presence**: `ViewModels/MainViewModelRichPresenceTests.cs` (a recording `IRichPresencePublisher` fake on `MainViewModel.RichPresence`: a join publishes name + `ARTCC · airport` back-dated by the room's elapsed seconds, a recording load publishes its own scenario, a late scenario name republishes, no scenario / preference off publishes nothing, leaving clears, the state line omits the missing half).

    `UserPreferencesDiscordRichPresenceTests.cs` (`DiscordRichPresenceEnabled` defaults on and round-trips)
  - **Speech telemetry**: `ViewModels/SpeechTelemetryPromptTests.cs` (first enable raises the opt-in offer once and accepting opts in, declining leaves speech on and telemetry off, an answered offer never re-raises, the on-open offer follows speech state, one dialog at a time).

    `ViewModels/SettingsViewModelSpeechTelemetryTests.cs` (saving telemetry on marks the offer answered and turns capture on; an untouched save leaves the offer due; on, Apply, off, Apply in one window leaves it off). `UserPreferencesSpeechTelemetryTests.cs` (telemetry defaults off and round-trips, forces capture on, prompt-shown round-trips)
  - **Settings window**: `Views/SettingsWindowSourceTests.cs` (every binding and named control of the tabbed window survives in the sections; every Speech action that runs at once says Cancel doesn't undo it). `Views/SettingsWindowNavigationTests.cs` (one selectable sidebar row per section, `SelectSection` shows each section's view, section links open their target, rows carry automation ids and headers cannot be selected).

    `Views/SettingsDialogHostTests.cs` (Apply refreshes the live views and Cancel rolls back only what came after it, OK commits and closes, Tools → Settings opens on General and the pilot-voice request on Speech, a Speech Debug request while Settings is open moves the open window to Speech).

    `Views/SettingsKeyCaptureTests.cs` (the Quick bookmark key captures a combo and ends capture; Enter/Escape/Space are captured while capturing, and otherwise Enter applies and closes, Escape closes without applying).

    `Views/SettingsTryItOutTests.cs` (Enter in Try it out leaves the window open and applies nothing). `ViewModels/SettingsViewModelApplyTests.cs` (an applied value survives a later uncommitted edit; Apply writes only the always-on-top values that changed).

    `ViewModels/SoloAdvisorySettingsTests.cs` (the solo advisory pre-fill and live check: defaults, preference and bundle round trips, staging in Settings, the session copy never writing the preferences, the reseed on room join, scenario load and recording load but not on a rejoin of the same room or a session-settings update, and the flyout showing them only in solo).

    `ViewModels/SettingsViewModelResetSectionTests.cs` (one test per section: Reset section shows the defaults and the preferences change only on Apply; General keeps the initials; link-only sections disable it; Cancel after a reset changes nothing). `UserPreferencesDefaultsTests.cs` (`CreateDefaults` reads the fresh-file defaults, not the user's file, and a setter on it throws).

    `ViewModels/SettingsViewModelQuickCommandsTests.cs` (the Quick commands section's staged lists: add, remove, move, resets, validation, the Add command… offer, Apply writes only changed situations). `Views/QuickCommandsSectionTests.cs` (the section's entry order, drag drops, the preview, the flyout's Add, the flight-rules caption and the invalid-row warning).

    `Helpers/PreferencesFilePerTestAttribute.cs` (assembly-level `BeforeAfterTestAttribute`: every test starts on a fresh, empty `preferences.json` under the per-process root, via `UserPreferences.UseFile`), `Helpers/MethodNameOrderer.cs` (ordinal method-name `ITestCaseOrderer`), `Views/PreferencesIsolationTests.cs` (a hidden window's preferences and debounced geometry save from one test never reach the next).

    `Helpers/ScopeTestFixture.cs` (the Scope tests' shared setup: `MainAsync`, `OpenScopeAsync` and an exact-position `AddAircraft`; a test file reaches them with `using static Yaat.Client.UI.Tests.Helpers.ScopeTestFixture;`). Reach for it before writing another private copy.
  - **Scenario load overlay**: `ViewModels/LoadOverlayViewModelTests.cs` (a stale `Sequence` and another load's events are dropped, an all-`done`/`notNeeded` table closes itself, a `warning` or `failed` step keeps it open until Close, the RPC result's `Steps` alone renders the final table, a refusal closes it).

    `ViewModels/MainViewModelLoadOverlayTests.cs` (progress events reach the overlay; `IsRoomLoading` from `RoomLoadingBy` or the client's own load disables Load, Unload, Restart and the rewinds and raises their can-execute changes; the status line while and after a member's load; `RewindToSeconds` sends nothing mid-load)
  - **Departure auto-delete**: `UserPreferencesDepartureAutoDeleteTests.cs` (a preferences file without `departureAutoDeleteDistanceNm` reads null; a set distance persists and null clears it), `ViewModels/MainViewModelSessionSettingsTests.cs` (the session-flyout box follows `DepartureAutoDeleteDistanceNm` from `SessionSettingsDto` and the load result, null blanking it)
  - **Startup loads**: `ViewModels/MainViewModelStartupLoadTests.cs` (the view model's three constructor loads run through `MainViewModel.StartupLoads`). `ModuleInit.StartupLoads.cs` implements the `InstallStartupLoads` partial hook that installs `Fakes/OfflineStartupLoads.cs` (the committed test navdata set off the constructor, no vNAS config request, no update check); assemblies that link only `ModuleInit.cs` compile the hook away.
  - **Session-flyout debounce**: `ViewModels/MainViewModelSessionSettingsTests.cs` on a `Helpers/ManualTimeProvider.cs` clock (typed digits send the last value once after 1 s idle, steps after 200 ms, the departure distance never on idle, flush and discard, a push keeps a pending typed value and the untouched half of a pair follows the push), `Views/CommandInputViewTests.cs` (Enter, focus loss, spinner step and flyout close reach the committer)
- **UI render tests**: `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Render.Tests/` — headless tests that need real pixels (`UseHeadlessDrawing = false` + Skia, as yaat-server's `tools/Yaat.GuideCapture`), a separate assembly because the headless setting is per-assembly; `Automation/AutomationScreenshotTests.cs` (the pipe's `screenshot`: window and element captures at the render scale, overlay popups included).

  It links `AutomationHostFixture.cs`, `AutomationPipeTestClient.cs` and `ModuleInit.cs` from `Yaat.Client.UI.Tests` rather than copying them.
- **Precompute cache tool tests**: `tests/Yaat.PrecomputeCache.Tests/CliTests.cs` runs `PrecomputeCacheCli.RunAsync` against a stubbed vNAS (usage exit 2, a map-less airport, a cached-only NavData serial, one failed airport not stopping the run, `--check` warnings and exit codes, `--refresh-airports` failures); the check, scenario-airport and entry-build rules are in `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/Data/Airport/Precompute/` (`PrecomputeBuilderTests.cs` for `Check` and `RefreshEnvelopes`, `PrecomputeBuilderBuildTests.cs`, `PrecomputeBuilderParallelTests.cs`, `PrecomputeBuilderFixtures.cs` for the shared fixture, `ScenarioAirportCollectorTests.cs`).
- **Client-driver MCP tests**: live in yaat-server (`tests/Yaat.ClientDriver.Mcp.Tests/`); they link this repo's `tests/Yaat.Client.UI.Tests/Helpers/AutomationHostFixture.cs`, `AutomationPipeTestClient.cs` and `InteractiveDesktop.cs`.

  `ActionLogTests.cs` (`RecordingSession.LogActionAsync` while recording writes the header then one LF-terminated line per click/hover/drag of the recorded pid and none for another pid; actions before the first frame get a null `startedUtc` and `clipSeconds` that stop fills in; an action stamped while recording is logged after the stop; nothing recording writes no file).

  `BatchActionLogTests.cs` (batch_drive click and drag steps while recording log the lines the standalone tools log, with the window's render scaling in the header; an invoke step writes none).
  `ElementRegistryTests.cs` (UIA and pipe ids share one sequence, a pipe node keeps its id, a pipe id is refused by the UIA resolver). `InspectToolsPipeTests.cs` (list_windows, dump_tree, find_elements and get_value on a YAAT pipe: row shapes and window-relative rects, the 400-line cap, descendants only, exact Avalonia types, automationId falling back to x:Name, an empty TextBox reading "", a stale id, the UIA fallback when the cached client was evicted).

  `InputToolsPipeTests.cs` (click, invoke, click_point, set_text, send_keys, focus and set_input_mode on a YAAT pipe: the semantic action named, modifiers and double-click reaching the pointer, disabled / gone / not-focusable / out-of-bounds errors, keys with no element going to the remembered pipe pid, a UIA window id refused for click_point).

  `PipeDescribeTests.cs` (row formatting edge cases). `PipeCallsTests.cs` (a stale node, another host error, a closed or missing pipe, routing with and without a discovery file, the remembered pid kept, recorded and cleared).

  `BatchToolsTests.cs` (batch_drive over a stub pipe: step order, first-failure stop, refusals, assert wait and no_errors, the answer timeout and the 300 s deadline, caller cancellation). `ClientErrorsTests.cs` (the formatted client-errors block lists every entry with its exception; a tool call's result carries the block on a success and an error answer and stays unchanged without client errors).

  `WaitUntilToolTests.cs` (wait_until sends conditions, mode and the clamped timeout, the request timeout covers the host wait, met=false is a result not an error, the screenshot is saved and its path returned, an unsavable screenshot is a line, then-action results and a failed action without a reason are reported, the ceiling matches the host's).

  `RecorderLocatorTests.cs` (the recorder copied under `recorder/` exists and answers `--help`). `RecordToolsTests.cs` (record_start/mark/stop over a fake backend: target resolution, refusals, defaults, marks file, a died pipeline, the stop summary, wait_until's stop_recording).

  `FfmpegPipelineTests.cs` (the recorder and ffmpeg arguments, quoting, encoder listing). `ClientAreaCropTests.cs` (the crop at any title-bar offset, even sides). `RecordingFakes.cs` (the fake backend and process). `TestAppBuilder.cs` (the headless Avalonia app)
- **LayoutInspector tests**: `tests/Yaat.LayoutInspector.Tests/` — CLI option parsing and tick-recording merge. `CliOptionsTicksTests.cs` (`--ticks` is repeatable, `[LABEL=]<path>` split only when the text before `=` has no path separator or `.`, repeated flags keep their order, no `--ticks` leaves `TickSources` empty). `CliOptionsVideoMapTests.cs` (`--videomap <path>` sets the path, no flag leaves it null, and loading OAK with its tower-cab videomap moves the hold-short bars). `ArcsCommandTests.cs` (`--arcs` / `--arcs-max-radius` parsing, a non-number rejected, OAK's arcs listed tightest first, the radius cap).

  `TickRecordingMergerTests.cs` (`TickRecordingMerger.Merge`: a LABEL renames a one-aircraft run's callsign to the label and a multi-aircraft run's to `LABEL:CALLSIGN`, merged aircraft get palette colours while a single unlabelled recording keeps its own, ticks are ordered by time with source order preserved on ties, and it throws `TickMergeException` on a shared unlabelled callsign or recordings from different airports)
- **Test data**: `tests/Yaat.Sim.Tests/TestData/` — NavData.dat + `navdata-manifest.json`, FAACIFP18.gz + `cifp-manifest.json`, airport GeoJSON (every vNAS training airport with a taxi graph, listed in `layout-airports.txt`; `TowerCabMaps/oak.geojson`: the OAK tower-cab videomap, in a subfolder because tests read every top-level `*.geojson` as a layout), `oak-u-w-fillet-corner-recording.zip` (E2E fillet routing test fixture).

  `oak-push-then-taxi-approach-recording.zip` (DAL2150 PUSH-then-TAXI off OAK gate 15, for the approach-leg replay test). `s2-oak5-follow-heli-recording.zip` (S2-OAK-5 bundle: FOLLOW behind a straight-in jet, helicopter LAND @spot from off-field, CRC flight-plan amendment during a hover hold).

  `ual58-spot9-reversal-recording.yaat-bug-report-bundle.zip` (UAL58 B77W off SFO gate G10: `TAXI T9 $9` to spot 9, then `TAXI A F 28L` reversing onto taxiway A — the entry-alignment reversal fixture for `Ual58Spot9ReversalTests`).

  `yaat1262-sfo-28r-exit-q-recording.yaat-bug-report-bundle.zip` (an arrival exiting SFO 28R onto Q — the 28L auto pull-up fixture for `SfoExitQParallelPullUpTests`). `recording-routing-census.json` (per-fixture routing census of every recorded command — the triage worklist for the action-router work).

  Refresh pins: `tools/refresh-navdata.py` (NavData and CIFP together, to the NavData's cycle; tests pin these committed files, never the user cache or the network), airport GeoJSON via `tools/refresh-test-layouts.py`.
- **Shared loader**: `TestVnasData.EnsureInitialized()` — always use this, never synthetic stubs

## Deep docs

The Task Index tells you *which files*; these docs explain *how each subsystem works*, and a doc that owns files lists them under `## File map`. Read the relevant one before a non-trivial change. The annotated list with summaries is [README.md](README.md), Subsystem docs.

| Area | Deep-dive doc(s) |

| Command input (autocomplete / signature help) | [command-input-ux.md](command-input-ux.md) |
